# 04 — Legacy Stored Procedure Map

> **Total Unique Stored Procedures**: **1855**
> **Internal SQL Body Status**: `UNKNOWN — EVIDENCE NOT AVAILABLE` (Stored procedure bodies reside in the live MySQL `brahmainsurance` database and are not present in repository source files; parameter contracts, return patterns, and callers are 100% confirmed from C# ADO.NET bindings).

## 2. Parameter Convention & Return Pattern Analysis

1. **Parameter Naming**:
   - Almost all stored procedures prefix parameters with `P_` (e.g., `P_CustomerId`, `P_RegistrationNo`, `P_TransanctionId`, `P_Opr`) or `_` / `p_`.
2. **Operation Multiplexing (`P_Opr` / `P_Flag` / `P_Type`)**:
   - Single stored procedures frequently multiplex `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, and `SELECT` operations using a discriminator parameter:
     - `usp_CustomerOperation` / `usp_VehicleDetailsOperation` / `usp_TransactionOperation`
     - `usp_tdsforagent` (`P_Opr` = `"INS"`, `"UPD"`, `"SEL"`)
     - `usp_tdsforbroker` (`P_Opr` = `"insert"`, `"Update"`)
     - `usp_UpdateAgentCommissionPay` (`P_Flag` = `"AgentCommProcess"`, `"AgentCommPay"`, `"OnlinePayment"`, `"PolicyCancel"`)
     - `usp_PaymentApproval` (`P_Type` = `"CASHIER"`, `"ACCOUNTANT"`, `"OWNER"`, `"ONLINETORA"`)
     - `usp_VehicleHistory` (`P_Type` = `"REG"`, `"PREREC"`, `"DONE_PREREC"`, `"CASHAPPROVED"`, `"BRANCHCASH"`, `"DONE_BRANCHCASH"`, `"CMPOUT"`, `"DONE_CMPOUT"`, `"CUSTCHEQUE"`, `"ACC"`)
3. **Output / Return IDs**:
   - Insert procedures either return a `SELECT LAST_INSERT_ID()` scalar read via `ExecuteScalar()` / `DataTable.Rows[0][0]` or bind an `OUT` parameter `P_OutId` (`ParameterDirection.Output`).
